lineage: Qwen/Qwen3-4B-Base, Qwen/Qwen3-4B, Qwen/Qwen3-4B-SafeRL (the\nofficial safety RL model, not the instruct one), and an abliterated Qwen3-4B. instruct, saferl\nand abliterated share a chat template so they are directly comparable, base uses a different\nformat so keep it separate. poke around open ended, look at weights and activations, see what\nactually differs between the four.\n\nstep 2 - design 50 metrics. informed by what you found in step 1, but also by the literature -\nsafety papers and mech interp papers in general, not only safety ones. include a few black-box\nmetrics too, things that only read logits or output text, like the logit-gap margin, so we have\na comparison point for whether looking inside the model actually buys anything.\n\nstep 3 - test all 50 much wider. other lineages, pairs and triplets where a safety-tuned or\nabliterated sibling exists, and standalone models where none does. for each metric: does it\nseparate safe vs normal vs abliterated. hold out a set of models that no metric is tuned on,\nbecause picking the best of 50 on the models you designed them on is cheating.\n\nstep 4 - ground truth. pull real benchmark numbers from official sources, model cards, papers,\nleaderboards, not just your own judge. safety is not only refusal - try to cover other aspects\ntoo, see TrustLLM and AIR-Bench for what that means. if that turns out to be too much, then two\nseparate refusal rates is acceptable as a fallback: refusal on harmful prompts, and refusal on\nharmless prompts that only look dangerous (xstest style). either way a model that refuses\neverything must lose, not win. also pull capability benchmarks, gsm8k, mmlu, arena-hard, to\nsee whether safety trades off against performance. do not use Qwen3Guard as a judge for SafeRL,\nit was SafeRL's training reward.\n\nstep 5 - take the 10 best metrics and correlation-test them against those benchmark numbers.\nreport the resampling unit and both aggregation units. a metric that only works within one\narchitecture family is a negative result, say so.\n\nbonus - if a metric works really well, mech interp analysis of why. what is it Adding models, datasets, or settings to an experiment that\nalready ran makes the table bigger; it does not make the contribution\nstronger, and it is the default a strategy generator drifts into when it has\nnothing sharper to propose. Spend an artifact on scale only when the SPREAD\nitself is the finding (a scaling trend, a regime boundary, a generalisation\nclaim the paper actually makes). Otherwise spend it on something that could\nchange the conclusion: the mechanism behind an observed effect, the condition\nunder which it disappears, the confound that would explain it away, or the\nbaseline whose absence a reviewer would name first.\n\n{\n        f'''Generate {num_strategies} COMPLEMENTARY strategies that will ALL run simultaneously.\n\n**CRITICAL — COMPLEMENTARY, NOT CONFLICTING:**\n- Each strategy should approach the hypothesis from a DIFFERENT angle or explore a different facet\n- Strategies must NOT duplicate effort — no two strategies should plan the same artifact for the same purpose\n- Together they should cover more ground than a single strategy could alone\n- Think of them as {num_strategies} research teams working simultaneously — how would you split the work so each team's output makes the others' more valuable?'''\n        if num_strategies > 1\n        else \"\"\n    }\n</task>\"\"\"\n\n\n# =============================================================================\n# HELPERS\n# =============================================================================\n\n\ndef _format_compute_hardware(\n    compute_profiles: dict,\n    artifact_type_profiles: dict[str, list[str]],\n    allowed_artifacts: list[str] | None = None,\n) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Format the REAL hardware each artifact type executes on.\n\n    gen_strat's own agent session runs on a cheap planning pod (this session\n    may have shell/hardware-detection tool access), and it is NOT the pod an\n    experiment/dataset/evaluation artifact executes on later — those get\n    their own pod per ``artifact_type_profiles`` (e.g. GPU), assigned when\n    the artifact's plan is created downstream. Without this block the\n    strategist has no way to tell the two apart and can wrongly size the\n    strategy's scale against its own shell's hardware.\n    \"\"\"\n    types = allowed_artifacts or list(artifact_type_profiles.keys())\n    lines = [\n        \"This planning session's own shell (if you inspect it, e.g. via \"\n        \"aii-use-hardware or nproc) is a lightweight pod and is NOT what any \"\n        \"artifact executes on. Each artifact you direct runs LATER on its \"\n        \"own separately-provisioned pod, sized by artifact type:\",\n        \"\",\n    ]\n    any_line = False\n    for artifact_type in types:\n        allowed = artifact_type_profiles.get(artifact_type, [])\n        if not allowed:\n            continue\n        descs = []\n        for name in allowed:\n            profile = compute_profiles.get(name)\n            desc = getattr(profile, \"description\", \"\") if profile is not None else \"\"\n            descs.append(f\"{name} ({desc})\" if desc else name)\n        lines.append(f\"  - {artifact_type}: {', '.join(descs)}\")\n        any_line = True\n    if not any_line:\n        return \"\"\n    lines.append(\"\")\n    lines.append(\n        \"Size scale decisions (panel/sweep counts, model counts, etc.) against \"\n        \"these tiers, not against this planning session's own hardware.\"\n    )\n    return \"\\n\".join(lines)\n\n\n#: Enough alternates that the hypothesis step judged the request OPEN. The\n#: gen_hypo prompt asks for 3-4 runner-ups when the choice of contribution was\n#: left to the model and 2 brief ones when the request prescribed method,\n#: deliverable or comparison — so the COUNT is that judgement already made,\n#: read back here rather than re-derived from the request text (which this\n#: step never sees in full).\n_OPEN_ASK_ALTERNATES = 3\n\n\ndef _wants_wide_screen(hypothesis: dict, current_iteration: int, alternates: list) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Is this iteration a wide screen rather than a deep test?\n\n    Two ways in. Either ``upd_hypo`` decided ``widen`` last round — the\n    explicit request, and the one the whole steering rule exists to deliver —\n    or this is the first iteration of an open-ended ask that already carries a\n    candidate population, where screening before committing is cheaper than\n    discovering on iteration 2 that the one claim in play was the wrong one.\n    \"\"\"\n    if str(hypothesis.get(\"_move\") or \"\") == \"widen\":\n        return True\n    return current_iteration <= 1 and len(alternates) >= _OPEN_ASK_ALTERNATES\n\n\ndef _build_previous_strategy_section(previous_strategies: list[dict]) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Build previous strategy section from all strategies of the previous iteration.\"\"\"\n    if not previous_strategies:\n        return \"\"\"<previous_strategies>\nNo previous strategies exist. This is the FIRST iteration.\n</previous_strategies>\"\"\"\n\n    strategies_text = to_prompt_yaml_list(previous_strategies, label=\"Strategy\")\n\n    return f\"\"\"<previous_strategies>\nStrategies from the PREVIOUS iteration. You can CONTINUE these directions,\nADAPT based on what worked and what didn't in the artifacts produced, or PIVOT if results suggest a better path.\n\n{strategies_text}\n</previous_strategies>\"\"\"\n\n\n# =============================================================================\n# EXPORTS\n# =============================================================================\n\n\ndef get(\n    hypothesis: dict,\n    artifacts: list[BaseArtifact],\n    current_iteration: int,\n    max_iterations: int,\n    previous_strategies: list[dict] | None = None,\n    *,\n    allowed_artifacts: list[str] | None = None,\n    num_strategies: int = 1,\n    art_limit: int | None = None,\n    artifact_context_per_type: int = 10,\n    reviewer_feedback_text: str | None = None,\n    paper_text: str | None = None,\n    user_folder_path: str = \"\",\n    compute_profiles: dict | None = None,\n    artifact_type_profiles: dict[str, list[str]] | None = None,\n) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Build user prompt for strategy generation.\"\"\"\n    hypo_filtered = hypothesis_for_prompt(hypothesis)\n\n    remaining = max_iterations - current_iteration + 1\n\n    alternates = hypo_filtered.get(\"alternates\") or []\n    wide_screen = _wants_wide_screen(hypothesis, current_iteration, alternates)\n    alternates_text = to_prompt_yaml_list(alternates, label=\"Candidate\") if alternates else \"\"\n\n    compute_hardware_text = \"\"\n    if compute_profiles and artifact_type_profiles:\n        compute_hardware_text = _format_compute_hardware(\n            compute_profiles, artifact_type_profiles, allowed_artifacts\n        )\n\n    prompt = PROMPT(\n        hypothesis_text=to_prompt_yaml(hypo_filtered),\n        current_iteration=current_iteration,\n        max_iterations=max_iterations,\n        remaining=remaining,\n        previous_strategy_section=_build_previous_strategy_section(previous_strategies or []),\n        artifact_planning_guidance=get_artifact_planning(allowed_artifacts),\n        artifact_types_text=get_artifact_context(allowed_artifacts),\n        artifact_scope_text=get_artifact_scope(allowed_artifacts),\n        existing_artifacts_text=LLMPromptModel.list_to_prompt_yaml(\n            artifacts,\n            label=\"Item\",\n            include={\n                \"id\",\n                \"type\",\n                \"title\",\n                \"summary\",\n                \"out_dependency_files\",\n                \"workspace_path\",\n                \"out_expected_files\",\n            },\n            strip_nulls=True,\n        ),\n        narratives_text=paper_text or \"\",\n        num_strategies=num_strategies,\n        art_limit=art_limit,\n        reviewer_feedback_text=reviewer_feedback_text or \"\",\n        compute_hardware_text=compute_hardware_text,\n        wide_screen=wide_screen,\n        alternates_text=alternates_text,\n    )\n    return prompt + get_user_folder_prompt(user_folder_path) + get_user_request_prompt()\n\n\n# =============================================================================\n# RETRY PROMPT BUILDERS (for artifact verification failures)\n# =============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_artifact_retry_prompt(\n    verification: dict,\n    num_strategies_requested: int,\n    min_valid_artifacts: int | None = None,\n    art_limit: int | None = None,\n) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Build retry prompt for artifact verification failures.\n\n    Reports ALL problems (limit, deps, types, etc.) in a single prompt so the LLM\n    can fix everything at once.\n\n    Note: ID errors no longer occur since IDs are code-assigned after LLM output.\n\n    Args:\n        verification: Dict from verify_strategies()\n        num_strategies_requested: How many strategies were requested\n        min_valid_artifacts: Minimum valid artifacts required (optional, for error message)\n        art_limit: Max artifact directions per strategy (optional, for error message)\n\n    Returns:\n        Retry prompt string explaining ALL issues and requesting fixes\n    \"\"\"\n    lines = [\n        \"<verification_results>\",\n        \"Your previous response had issues that need fixing:\",\n        \"\",\n    ]\n\n    count_errors = verification.get(\"count_errors\", [])\n    dep_errors = verification.get(\"dep_errors\", [])\n    type_errors = verification.get(\"type_errors\", [])\n    limit_errors = verification.get(\"limit_errors\", [])\n    strategies_received = verification.get(\"strategies_received\", 0)\n    valid_artifact_count = verification.get(\"valid_artifact_count\", 0)\n    total_artifact_count = verification.get(\"total_artifact_count\", 0)\n\n    # Count mismatch\n    if count_errors:\n        lines.append(\"STRATEGY COUNT ERROR:\")\n        lines.append(f\"  Requested: {num_strategies_requested} strategies\")\n        lines.append(f\"  Received: {strategies_received} strategies\")\n        lines.append(\"\")\n\n    # Artifact limit exceeded\n    if limit_errors:\n        lines.append(f\"ARTIFACT LIMIT EXCEEDED (max {art_limit} artifact directions per strategy):\")\n        for err in limit_errors:\n            lines.append(f\"  - {err}\")\n        lines.append(\"\")\n\n    # Type errors (artifact type not in allowed_artifacts)\n    if type_errors:\n        lines.append(\"ARTIFACT TYPE ERRORS (only certain artifact types are allowed):\")\n        for err in type_errors:\n            lines.append(f\"  - {err}\")\n        lines.append(\"\")\n\n    # Dependency errors\n    if dep_errors:\n        lines.append(\n            \"DEPENDENCY ERRORS (depends_on can ONLY reference IDs from <existing_artifacts>):\"\n        )\n        for err in dep_errors:\n            lines.append(f\"  - {err}\")\n        lines.append(\"\")\n\n    # Valid artifact count (if min_valid_artifacts check failed)\n    if min_valid_artifacts is not None and valid_artifact_count < min_valid_artifacts:\n        lines.append(\"INSUFFICIENT VALID ARTIFACTS:\")\n        lines.append(f\"  Required: at least {min_valid_artifacts} valid artifacts\")\n        lines.append(f\"  Found: {valid_artifact_count} valid out of {total_artifact_count} total\")\n        lines.append(\n            \"  Artifacts with invalid types, duplicate IDs, or invalid dependencies don't count as valid.\"\n        )\n        lines.append(\"\")\n\n    lines.append(\"</verification_results>\")\n    lines.append(\"\")\n    lines.append(\"<task>\")\n    lines.append(\"Fix ALL issues above and regenerate your strategies:\")\n    lines.append(\"\")\n\n    step_num = 1\n    if count_errors:\n        lines.append(\n            f\"{step_num}. Generate EXACTLY {num_strategies_requested} strategies (you provided {strategies_received})\"\n        )\n        step_num += 1\n\n    if limit_errors:\n        lines.append(f\"{step_num}. Reduce artifact directions to AT MOST {art_limit} per strategy:\")\n        lines.append(f\"   - Keep only the {art_limit} highest-impact artifacts per strategy\")\n        lines.append(\"   - Remove or merge lower-priority artifacts\")\n        step_num += 1\n\n    if type_errors:\n        lines.append(f\"{step_num}. JSON with the fixed strategies.\")\n    lines.append(\"</task>\")\n\n    return \"\\n\".join(lines)\n======SCHEMA======\n\"\"\"Schemas for strategy generation — pool objects and LLM wrapper for structured output\n\nUtility:\n- assign_artifact_direction_ids: Assigns unique IDs to artifact directions after LLM output\n- validate_artifact_dependencies: Validates dependencies exist and follow type rules\n- DEPENDENCY_RULES: Mapping of artifact type -> (required_dep_types, optional_dep_types)\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom typing import Annotated, Literal\n\nfrom aii_lib.prompts import LLMPrompt, LLMPromptModel, LLMStructOut, LLMStructOutModel\nfrom pydantic import Field\n\n# =============================================================================\n# SCHEMAS\n# =============================================================================\n\n\nclass ArtifactDep(LLMPromptModel, LLMStructOutModel):\n    \"\"\"A single dependency on an existing artifact, with a short type label.\n\n    ``id`` and ``label`` are LLM-generated at strategy time. ``label`` is free-text but\n    short — a word or two naming the type of dependency, not a sentence.\n\n    ``relation_type`` and ``relation_rationale`` are populated later, in upd_hypo,\n    using the MultiCite citation-function typology (Lauscher et al., NAACL 2022).\n    They are absent at strategy time and may stay absent for legacy runs.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    id: Annotated[str, LLMPrompt, LLMStructOut] = Field(\n        description=\"ID of an existing artifact this artifact depends on\"\n    )\n    label: Annotated[str, LLMPrompt, LLMStructOut] = Field(\n        description=\"Short free-text label naming the type of this dependency (a word or two, not a sentence)\"\n    )\n    relation_type: str | None = Field(\n        default=None,\n        description=\"MultiCite type set in upd_hypo: background/motivation/uses/extends/similarities/differences\",\n    )\n    relation_rationale: str | None = Field(\n        default=None,\n        description=\"Brief rationale (≤120 chars) set in upd_hypo\",\n    )\n\n\nclass ArtifactDirection(LLMPromptModel, LLMStructOutModel):\n    \"\"\"High-level direction for an artifact to execute this iteration.\n\n    ID is code-assigned (LLMPrompt only — visible in prompts, not LLM-generated).\n    \"\"\"\n\n    id: Annotated[str, LLMPrompt] = Field(\n        default=\"\",\n        description=\"Unique artifact ID assigned by code (e.g., 'experiment_iter1_dir1')\",\n    )\n    type: Annotated[\n        Literal[\"experiment\", \"research\", \"proof\", \"evaluation\", \"dataset\"],\n        LLMPrompt,\n        LLMStructOut,\n    ] = Field(description=\"Type of artifact to create\")\n    objective: Annotated[str, LLMPrompt, LLMStructOut] = Field(\n        description=\"What we want to achieve with this artifact\"\n    )\n    approach: Annotated[str, LLMPrompt, LLMStructOut] = Field(\n        description=\"High-level direction/method\"\n    )\n    depends_on: Annotated[list[ArtifactDep], LLMPrompt, LLMStructOut] = Field(\n        default_factory=list,\n        description=\"Existing artifacts this depends on, each with a short type label\",\n    )\n\n\nclass Strategy(LLMPromptModel, LLMStructOutModel):\n    \"\"\"A research strategy.\n\n    Content fields have LLMPrompt + LLMStructOut markers.\n    ``id`` is code-assigned (LLMPrompt only — visible in prompts, not LLM-generated).\n\n    ID format: gen_strat_idx{N}\n    \"\"\"\n\n    kind: Literal[\"strategy\"] = \"strategy\"\n    id: Annotated[str, LLMPrompt] = Field(\n        default=\"\", description=\"Unique strategy ID (e.g., gen_strat_idx1)\"\n    )\n    title: Annotated[str, LLMPrompt, LLMStructOut] = Field(\n        description=\"Strategy name in plain, everyday language — short and jargon-free so a non-expert grasps it at a glance and it fits the run visualizations. Aim for about 4-8 words (~40 characters).\"\n    )\n    objective: Annotated[str, LLMPrompt, LLMStructOut] = Field(\n        description=\"The novel contribution we're building toward\"\n    )\n    rationale: Annotated[str, LLMPrompt, LLMStructOut] = Field(\n        description=\"Why this strategy is promising\"\n    )\n    artifact_directions: Annotated[list[ArtifactDirection], LLMPrompt, LLMStructOut] = Field(\n        description=\"Artifacts to execute THIS iteration\"\n    )\n    expected_outcome: Annotated[str, LLMPrompt, LLMStructOut] = Field(\n        description=\"What we'll have after this iteration's artifacts complete\"\n    )\n    summary: Annotated[str, LLMPrompt, LLMStructOut] = Field(\n        default=\"\",\n        description=\"Brief summary of the strategy and its expected contribution\",\n    )\n\n\nclass Strategies(LLMPromptModel, LLMStructOutModel):\n    \"\"\"Top-level wrapper for LLM strategy generation output.\"\"\"\n\n    kind: Literal[\"strategies\"] = \"strategies\"\n    \"\"\"Wire-level discriminator for downstream consumers that narrow\n    polymorphic outputs by ``kind``. No LLMStructOut annotation:\n    invisible to the LLM's structured-output schema, populated via\n    the default at deserialize time.\"\"\"\n\n    strategies: Annotated[list[Strategy], LLMPrompt, LLMStructOut] = Field(\n        description=\"List of generated strategies\"\n    )\n\n\nclass GenStratOut(LLMPromptModel):\n    \"\"\"Aggregate output of gen_strat module with all strategies.\n\n    Every Strategy produced across the parallel tasks in one iteration.\n    Used as the typed payload for ``module_output(output=...)``; readers\n    walk ``module.output.strategies``.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    kind: Literal[\"gen_strat_out\"] = \"gen_strat_out\"\n    strategies: list[Strategy] = Field(default_factory=list)\n\n\n# =============================================================================\n# UTILITY: Assign artifact IDs\n# =============================================================================\n\n\ndef assign_artifact_direction_ids(\n    strategy: dict,\n    seen_ids: set[str],\n    iteration: int,\n) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Assign unique IDs to artifact directions.\n\n    The LLM does not generate IDs - we assign them sequentially based on type.\n    IDs are like 'experiment_iter1_dir1'.\n\n    IMPORTANT: Mutates seen_ids — new IDs are added so subsequent calls\n    (e.g., for other strategies in the same batch) won't generate duplicates.\n    Callers should pass a working copy if they need the original set unchanged.\n\n    Args:\n        strategy: Single strategy dict from LLM output\n        seen_ids: Mutable set of all known IDs (pool + previously assigned). TWO SHAPES OF ITERATION - a DEEP TEST pushes one claim further; a WIDE SCREEN tests several candidate answers cheaply in parallel and confirms the survivor on held-out evidence. Read which one this iteration is from the hypothesis and the instructions in the user prompt, and build that shape. Never answer a widened hypothesis with one more deep test.\n6. NEVER SHRINK TO FIT - do not plan an iteration whose best possible outcome is a smaller, safer version of a claim that already came back weak. If the claim is in trouble, the strategy's job is to put better candidates in play, not to find a corner where the old one survives.\n7. DIAGNOSE BEFORE DECIDING - before each iteration, review what worked, what didn't, and why. Use that to choose what to try next. Gaps are action items, not conclusions.\n8. PLAN FOR DEPENDENCIES - if an artifact depends on another (e.g. experiments need datasets), ensure prerequisites exist first or plan them this iteration for the next\n</principles>\n\n{get_work_solo_reminder()}\"\"\"\n\n\n# =============================================================================\n# HELPERS\n# =============================================================================\n\n\ndef _get_combined_resource_keys(\n    allowed_artifacts: list[str] | None = None,\n) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"Get union of all resource keys needed for the allowed artifact types.\"\"\"\n    if allowed_artifacts is None:\n        allowed_artifacts = list(ARTIFACT_RESOURCES.keys())\n    combined: set[str] = set()\n    for art_type in allowed_artifacts:\n        combined |= ARTIFACT_RESOURCES.get(art_type, set())\n    return list(combined)\n\n\n# =============================================================================\n# EXPORTS\n# =============================================================================\n\n\ndef get(allowed_artifacts: list[str] | None = None) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Build system prompt for strategy generation.\"\"\"\n    resource_keys = _get_combined_resource_keys(allowed_artifacts)\n    return PROMPT(\n        context=get_aii_context(focus=\"gen_strat\"),\n        resources_text=get_resources_prompt(include=resource_keys)\n        if resource_keys\n        else get_resources_prompt(),\n    )\n======UPROMPT======\n\"\"\"User prompt for strategy generation (Step 3.1: GEN_STRAT).\n\nRead top-to-bottom to understand the full prompt structure.\n\nTwo SHAPES of iteration, not one. get_domain_handbooks_section\nfrom aii_pipeline.prompts.components.evidence_moves import WIDE_SCREEN_BLOCK\nfrom aii_pipeline.prompts.components.hypothesis_display import hypothesis_for_prompt\nfrom aii_pipeline.prompts.components.user_folder import get_user_folder_prompt\nfrom aii_pipeline.prompts.components.user_request import get_user_request_prompt\nfrom aii_pipeline.utils import LLMPromptModel, to_prompt_yaml, to_prompt_yaml_list\n\nif TYPE_CHECKING:\n    from aii_pipeline.prompts.steps._3_invention_loop._3_gen_art.out_schema import (\n        BaseArtifact,\n    )\n\n\n# =============================================================================\n# PROMPT TEMPLATE\n# =============================================================================\n\n\ndef PROMPT(\n    hypothesis_text: str,\n    current_iteration: int,\n    max_iterations: int,\n    remaining: int,\n    previous_strategy_section: str,\n    *,\n    artifact_planning_guidance: str,\n    artifact_types_text: str,\n    artifact_scope_text: str,\n    existing_artifacts_text: str,\n    narratives_text: str,\n    num_strategies: int,\n    art_limit: int | None = None,\n    reviewer_feedback_text: str = \"\",\n    compute_hardware_text: str = \"\",\n    wide_screen: bool = False,\n    alternates_text: str = \"\",\n) -> str:\n    return f\"\"\"<hypothesis>\nYour strategy should advance this hypothesis.\n\n{hypothesis_text}\n</hypothesis>\n\n{\n        get_domain_handbooks_section(\n            focus=\"study design, proper baselines, and the evaluation/validity norms this field demands\"\n        )\n    }\n\n<iteration_status>\nCurrent iteration: {current_iteration} of {max_iterations}\nRemaining (including this one): {remaining}\n</iteration_status>\n\n{\n        f'''<candidate_alternates>\nRunner-up answers to the same ask, carried from hypothesis generation. These are the\ncandidate population a wide screen draws on — treat them as real options, not as context.\n\n{alternates_text}\n</candidate_alternates>'''\n        if alternates_text\n        else \"\"\n    }\n\n{WIDE_SCREEN_BLOCK if wide_screen else \"\"}\n\n{previous_strategy_section}\n\n<dependency_rules>\n- depends_on is a list of objects {{id, label}} — each entry references an existing artifact and tags how it is being used\n- \"id\" can ONLY reference IDs from <existing_artifacts> — never IDs you are proposing (all new artifacts run in parallel)\n- \"label\" is a SHORT free-text type label (a word or two, NOT a sentence) describing what role the dep plays — e.g. \"dataset\", \"validates\", \"extends\", \"supersedes\". Required on every dep.\n- Setting depends_on provides the dependency's out_dependency_files to your artifact at execution time\n- If no suitable existing artifacts exist, use empty depends_on\n- New artifact IDs are assigned by the system after submission — do not invent IDs for your proposed artifacts\n</dependency_rules>\n\n<available_artifact_types>\nArtifact types you can plan. Use this to choose the right types for your strategy objectives.\n\n{artifact_types_text}\n</available_artifact_types>\n\n{\n        f'''<compute_hardware>\n{compute_hardware_text}\n</compute_hardware>'''\n        if compute_hardware_text\n        else \"\"\n    }\n\n{artifact_scope_text}\n\n{artifact_planning_guidance}\n\n<existing_artifacts>\n{existing_artifacts_text.strip() or \"None yet (first iteration).\"}\n</existing_artifacts>\n\n{\n        f'''<current_paper>\nThe current paper draft — represents the research story so far.\n\nUse this to understand what's working, what's not, and what gaps remain.\nGaps and weak results signal what to try differently — not what to conclude.\n\n{narratives_text}\n</current_paper>'''\n        if narratives_text\n        else \"\"\n    }\n\n{\n        f'''<reviewer_feedback>\nPaper reviewer feedback from the previous iteration. Your strategy MUST address these critiques.\nPrioritize major issues — these are the most impactful improvements to make.\n\n{reviewer_feedback_text}\n</reviewer_feedback>'''\n        if reviewer_feedback_text\n        else \"\"\n    }\n\n<task>\nGenerate {num_strategies} research {\n        \"strategy\" if num_strategies == 1 else \"strategies\"\n    } for THIS iteration.\n\n{\n        f\"**ARTIFACT LIMIT: Each strategy may contain AT MOST {art_limit} artifact directions.** Focus on the highest-impact artifacts. Quality over quantity.\"\n        if art_limit\n        else \"\"\n    }\n\nEach strategy should:\n1. Define a clear OBJECTIVE - what novel contribution we're building toward\n2. Plan artifacts to execute NOW - specify type, objective, approach, and depends_on for each\n3. Account for parallel execution - all strategies and all planned artifacts run simultaneously, their artifacts are combined into one shared pool\n\n**BROADER IS NOT THE SAME AS DEEPER.** This applies when you are going DEEPER\non a claim that already has support — it is not an argument against a wide\nscreen, which tests DIFFERENT candidate answers rather than the same one in\nmore places. Adding models, datasets, or settings to an experiment that\nalready ran makes the table bigger; it does not make the contribution\nstronger, and it is the default a strategy generator drifts into when it has\nnothing sharper to propose. Spend an artifact on scale only when the SPREAD\nitself is the finding (a scaling trend, a regime boundary, a generalisation\nclaim the paper actually makes). Otherwise spend it on something that could\nchange the conclusion: the mechanism behind an observed effect, the condition\nunder which it disappears, the confound that would explain it away, or the\nbaseline whose absence a reviewer would name first.\n\n{\n        f'''Generate {num_strategies} COMPLEMENTARY strategies that will ALL run simultaneously.\n\n**CRITICAL — COMPLEMENTARY, NOT CONFLICTING:**\n- Each strategy should approach the hypothesis from a DIFFERENT angle or explore a different facet\n- Strategies must NOT duplicate effort — no two strategies should plan the same artifact for the same purpose\n- Together they should cover more ground than a single strategy could alone\n- Think of them as {num_strategies} research teams working simultaneously — how would you split the work so each team's output makes the others' more valuable?'''\n        if num_strategies > 1\n        else \"\"\n    }\n</task>\"\"\"\n\n\n# =============================================================================\n# HELPERS\n# =============================================================================\n\n\ndef _format_compute_hardware(\n    compute_profiles: dict,\n    artifact_type_profiles: dict[str, list[str]],\n    allowed_artifacts: list[str] | None = None,\n) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Format the REAL hardware each artifact type executes on.\n\n    gen_strat's own agent session runs on a cheap planning pod (this session\n    may have shell/hardware-detection tool access), and it is NOT the pod an\n    experiment/dataset/evaluation artifact executes on later — those get\n    their own pod per ``artifact_type_profiles`` (e.g. GPU), assigned when\n    the artifact's plan is created downstream. Without this block the\n    strategist has no way to tell the two apart and can wrongly size the\n    strategy's scale against its own shell's hardware.\n    \"\"\"\n    types = allowed_artifacts or list(artifact_type_profiles.keys())\n    lines = [\n        \"This planning session's own shell (if you inspect it, e.g. via \"\n        \"aii-use-hardware or nproc) is a lightweight pod and is NOT what any \"\n        \"artifact executes on. Each artifact you direct runs LATER on its \"\n        \"own separately-provisioned pod, sized by artifact type:\",\n        \"\",\n    ]\n    any_line = False\n    for artifact_type in types:\n        allowed = artifact_type_profiles.get(artifact_type, [])\n        if not allowed:\n            continue\n        descs = []\n        for name in allowed:\n            profile = compute_profiles.get(name)\n            desc = getattr(profile, \"description\", \"\") if profile is not None else \"\"\n            descs.append(f\"{name} ({desc})\" if desc else name)\n        lines.append(f\"  - {artifact_type}: {', '.join(descs)}\")\n        any_line = True\n    if not any_line:\n        return \"\"\n    lines.append(\"\")\n    lines.append(\n        \"Size scale decisions (panel/sweep counts, model counts, etc.) against \"\n        \"these tiers, not against this planning session's own hardware.\"\n    )\n    return \"\\n\".join(lines)\n\n\n#: Enough alternates that the hypothesis step judged the request OPEN. The\n#: gen_hypo prompt asks for 3-4 runner-ups when the choice of contribution was\n#: left to the model and 2 brief ones when the request prescribed method,\n#: deliverable or comparison — so the COUNT is that judgement already made,\n#: read back here rather than re-derived from the request text (which this\n#: step never sees in full).\n_OPEN_ASK_ALTERNATES = 3\n\n\ndef _wants_wide_screen(hypothesis: dict, current_iteration: int, alternates: list) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Is this iteration a wide screen rather than a deep test?\n\n    Two ways in. Either ``upd_hypo`` decided ``widen`` last round — the\n    explicit request, and the one the whole steering rule exists to deliver —\n    or this is the first iteration of an open-ended ask that already carries a\n    candidate population, where screening before committing is cheaper than\n    discovering on iteration 2 that the one claim in play was the wrong one.\n    \"\"\"\n    if str(hypothesis.get(\"_move\") or \"\") == \"widen\":\n        return True\n    return current_iteration <= 1 and len(alternates) >= _OPEN_ASK_ALTERNATES\n\n\ndef _build_previous_strategy_section(previous_strategies: list[dict]) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Build previous strategy section from all strategies of the previous iteration.\"\"\"\n    if not previous_strategies:\n        return \"\"\"<previous_strategies>\nNo previous strategies exist. This is the FIRST iteration.\n</previous_strategies>\"\"\"\n\n    strategies_text = to_prompt_yaml_list(previous_strategies, label=\"Strategy\")\n\n    return f\"\"\"<previous_strategies>\nStrategies from the PREVIOUS iteration. You can CONTINUE these directions,\nADAPT based on what worked and what didn't in the artifacts produced, or PIVOT if results suggest a better path.\n\n{strategies_text}\n</previous_strategies>\"\"\"\n\n\n# =============================================================================\n# EXPORTS\n# =============================================================================\n\n\ndef get(\n    hypothesis: dict,\n    artifacts: list[BaseArtifact],\n    current_iteration: int,\n    max_iterations: int,\n    previous_strategies: list[dict] | None = None,\n    *,\n    allowed_artifacts: list[str] | None = None,\n    num_strategies: int = 1,\n    art_limit: int | None = None,\n    artifact_context_per_type: int = 10,\n    reviewer_feedback_text: str | None = None,\n    paper_text: str | None = None,\n    user_folder_path: str = \"\",\n    compute_profiles: dict | None = None,\n    artifact_type_profiles: dict[str, list[str]] | None = None,\n) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Build user prompt for strategy generation.\"\"\"\n    hypo_filtered = hypothesis_for_prompt(hypothesis)\n\n    remaining = max_iterations - current_iteration + 1\n\n    alternates = hypo_filtered.get(\"alternates\") or []\n    wide_screen = _wants_wide_screen(hypothesis, current_iteration, alternates)\n    alternates_text = to_prompt_yaml_list(alternates, label=\"Candidate\") if alternates else \"\"\n\n    compute_hardware_text = \"\"\n    if compute_profiles and artifact_type_profiles:\n        compute_hardware_text = _format_compute_hardware(\n            compute_profiles, artifact_type_profiles, allowed_artifacts\n        )\n\n    prompt = PROMPT(\n        hypothesis_text=to_prompt_yaml(hypo_filtered),\n        current_iteration=current_iteration,\n        max_iterations=max_iterations,\n        remaining=remaining,\n        previous_strategy_section=_build_previous_strategy_section(previous_strategies or []),\n        artifact_planning_guidance=get_artifact_planning(allowed_artifacts),\n        artifact_types_text=get_artifact_context(allowed_artifacts),\n        artifact_scope_text=get_artifact_scope(allowed_artifacts),\n        existing_artifacts_text=LLMPromptModel.list_to_prompt_yaml(\n            artifacts,\n            label=\"Item\",\n            include={\n                \"id\",\n                \"type\",\n                \"title\",\n                \"summary\",\n                \"out_dependency_files\",\n                \"workspace_path\",\n                \"out_expected_files\",\n            },\n            strip_nulls=True,\n        ),\n        narratives_text=paper_text or \"\",\n        num_strategies=num_strategies,\n        art_limit=art_limit,\n        reviewer_feedback_text=reviewer_feedback_text or \"\",\n        compute_hardware_text=compute_hardware_text,\n        wide_screen=wide_screen,\n        alternates_text=alternates_text,\n    )\n    return prompt + get_user_folder_prompt(user_folder_path) + get_user_request_prompt()\n\n\n# =============================================================================\n# RETRY PROMPT BUILDERS (for artifact verification failures)\n# =============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_artifact_retry_prompt(\n    verification: dict,\n    num_strategies_requested: int,\n    min_valid_artifacts: int | None = None,\n    art_limit: int | None = None,\n) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Build retry prompt for artifact verification failures.\n\n    Reports ALL problems (limit, deps, types, etc.) in a single prompt so the LLM\n    can fix everything at once.\n\n    Note: ID errors no longer occur since IDs are code-assigned after LLM output.\n\n    Args:\n        verification: Dict from verify_strategies()\n        num_strategies_requested: How many strategies were requested\n        min_valid_artifacts: Minimum valid artifacts required (optional, for error message)\n        art_limit: Max artifact directions per strategy (optional, for error message)\n\n    Returns:\n        Retry prompt string explaining ALL issues and requesting fixes\n    \"\"\"\n    lines = [\n        \"<verification_results>\",\n        \"Your previous response had issues that need fixing:\",\n        \"\",\n    ]\n\n    count_errors = verification.get(\"count_errors\", [])\n    dep_errors = verification.get(\"dep_errors\", [])\n    type_errors = verification.get(\"type_errors\", [])\n    limit_errors = verification.get(\"limit_errors\", [])\n    strategies_received = verification.get(\"strategies_received\", 0)\n    valid_artifact_count = verification.get(\"valid_artifact_count\", 0)\n    total_artifact_count = verification.get(\"total_artifact_count\", 0)\n\n    # Count mismatch\n    if count_errors:\n        lines.append(\"STRATEGY COUNT ERROR:\")\n        lines.append(f\"  Requested: {num_strategies_requested} strategies\")\n        lines.append(f\"  Received: {strategies_received} strategies\")\n        lines.append(\"\")\n\n    # Artifact limit exceeded\n    if limit_errors:\n        lines.append(f\"ARTIFACT LIMIT EXCEEDED (max {art_limit} artifact directions per strategy):\")\n        for err in limit_errors:\n            lines.append(f\"  - {err}\")\n        lines.append(\"\")\n\n    # Type errors (artifact type not in allowed_artifacts)\n    if type_errors:\n        lines.append(\"ARTIFACT TYPE ERRORS (only certain artifact types are allowed):\")\n        for err in type_errors:\n            lines.append(f\"  - {err}\")\n        lines.append(\"\")\n\n    # Dependency errors\n    if dep_errors:\n        lines.append(\n            \"DEPENDENCY ERRORS (depends_on can ONLY reference IDs from <existing_artifacts>):\"\n        )\n        for err in dep_errors:\n            lines.append(f\"  - {err}\")\n        lines.append(\"\")\n\n    # Valid artifact count (if min_valid_artifacts check failed)\n    if min_valid_artifacts is not None and valid_artifact_count < min_valid_artifacts:\n        lines.append(\"INSUFFICIENT VALID ARTIFACTS:\")\n        lines.append(f\"  Required: at least {min_valid_artifacts} valid artifacts\")\n        lines.append(f\"  Found: {valid_artifact_count} valid out of {total_artifact_count} total\")\n        lines.append(\n            \"  Artifacts with invalid types, duplicate IDs, or invalid dependencies don't count as valid.\"\n        )\n        lines.append(\"\")\n\n    lines.append(\"</verification_results>\")\n    lines.append(\"\")\n    lines.append(\"<task>\")\n    lines.append(\"Fix ALL issues above and regenerate your strategies:\")\n    lines.append(\"\")\n\n    step_num = 1\n    if count_errors:\n        lines.append(\n            f\"{step_num}. Generate EXACTLY {num_strategies_requested} strategies (you provided {strategies_received})\"\n        )\n        step_num += 1\n\n    if limit_errors:\n        lines.append(f\"{step_num}. Reduce artifact directions to AT MOST {art_limit} per strategy:\")\n        lines.append(f\"   - Keep only the {art_limit} highest-impact artifacts per strategy\")\n        lines.append(\"   - Remove or merge lower-priority artifacts\")\n        step_num += 1\n\n    if type_errors:\n        lines.append(f\"{step_num}. Fix artifact type errors:\")\n        lines.append(\"   - Only use artifact types listed in <available_artifact_types>\")\n        lines.append(\"   - Check the allowed types and change your artifacts to use valid types\")\n        step_num += 1\n\n    if dep_errors:\n        lines.append(f\"{step_num}. Fix dependency errors:\")\n        lines.append(\n            \"   - depends_on is a list of {id, label} objects — every entry MUST have a non-empty short label\"\n        )\n        lines.append(\"   - id can ONLY reference IDs from <existing_artifacts>\")\n        lines.append(\n            \"   - You CANNOT reference artifacts you are proposing in this strategy as dependencies (they all run in parallel)\"\n        )\n        lines.append(\"   - Follow the dependency type rules (e.g., experiments require datasets)\")\n        lines.append(\"   - If no suitable existing artifacts exist, use depends_on: []\")\n        step_num += 1\n\n    if min_valid_artifacts is not None and valid_artifact_count < min_valid_artifacts:\n        lines.append(\n            f\"{step_num}. Ensure at least {min_valid_artifacts} artifacts are fully valid (correct types, no ID conflicts, valid dependencies)\"\n        )\n\n    lines.append(\"\")\n    lines.append(\"Output the corrected JSON with the fixed strategies.\")\n    lines.append(\"</task>\")\n\n    return \"\\n\".join(lines)\n======SCHEMA======\n\"\"\"Schemas for strategy generation — pool objects and LLM output.\n\nSchemas:\n- ArtifactDirection: High-level direction (id is code-assigned, excluded from LLM)\n- Strategy: Research strategy (id is code-assigned, excluded from LLM)\n- Strategies: Top-level wrapper for structured output\n\nUtility:\n- assign_artifact_direction_ids: Assigns unique IDs to artifact directions after LLM output\n- validate_artifact_dependencies: Validates dependencies exist and follow type rules\n- DEPENDENCY_RULES: Mapping of artifact type -> (required_dep_types, optional_dep_types)\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom typing import Annotated, Literal\n\nfrom aii_lib.prompts import LLMPrompt, LLMPromptModel, LLMStructOut, LLMStructOutModel\nfrom pydantic import Field\n\n# =============================================================================\n# SCHEMAS\n# =============================================================================\n\n\nclass ArtifactDep(LLMPromptModel, LLMStructOutModel):\n    \"\"\"A single dependency on an existing artifact, with a short type label.\n\n    ``id`` and ``label`` are LLM-generated at strategy time. ``label`` is free-text but\n    short — a word or two naming the type of dependency, not a sentence.\n\n    ``relation_type`` and ``relation_rationale`` are populated later, in upd_hypo,\n    using the MultiCite citation-function typology (Lauscher et al., NAACL 2022).\n    They are absent at strategy time and may stay absent for legacy runs.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    id: Annotated[str, LLMPrompt, LLMStructOut] = Field(\n        description=\"ID of an existing artifact this artifact depends on\"\n    )\n    label: Annotated[str, LLMPrompt, LLMStructOut] = Field(\n        description=\"Short free-text label naming the type of this dependency (a word or two, not a sentence)\"\n    )\n    relation_type: str | None = Field(\n        default=None,\n        description=\"MultiCite type set in upd_hypo: background/motivation/uses/extends/similarities/differences\",\n    )\n    relation_rationale: str | None = Field(\n        default=None,\n        description=\"Brief rationale (≤120 chars) set in upd_hypo\",\n    )\n\n\nclass ArtifactDirection(LLMPromptModel, LLMStructOutModel):\n    \"\"\"High-level direction for an artifact to execute this iteration.\n\n    ID is code-assigned (LLMPrompt only — visible in prompts, not LLM-generated).\n    \"\"\"\n\n    id: Annotated[str, LLMPrompt] = Field(\n        default=\"\",\n        description=\"Unique artifact ID assigned by code (e.g., 'experiment_iter1_dir1')\",\n    )\n    type: Annotated[\n        Literal[\"experiment\", \"research\", \"proof\", \"evaluation\", \"dataset\"],\n        LLMPrompt,\n        LLMStructOut,\n    ] = Field(description=\"Type of artifact to create\")\n    objective: Annotated[str, LLMPrompt, LLMStructOut] = Field(\n        description=\"What we want to achieve with this artifact\"\n    )\n    approach: Annotated[str, LLMPrompt, LLMStructOut] = Field(\n        description=\"High-level direction/method\"\n    )\n    depends_on: Annotated[list[ArtifactDep], LLMPrompt, LLMStructOut] = Field(\n        default_factory=list,\n        description=\"Existing artifacts this depends on, each with a short type label\",\n    )\n\n\nclass Strategy(LLMPromptModel, LLMStructOutModel):\n    \"\"\"A research strategy.\n\n    Content fields have LLMPrompt + LLMStructOut markers.\n    ``id`` is code-assigned (LLMPrompt only — visible in prompts, not LLM-generated).\n\n    ID format: gen_strat_idx{N}\n    \"\"\"\n\n    kind: Literal[\"strategy\"] = \"strategy\"\n    id: Annotated[str, LLMPrompt] = Field(\n        default=\"\", description=\"Unique strategy ID (e.g., gen_strat_idx1)\"\n    )\n    title: Annotated[str, LLMPrompt, LLMStructOut] = Field(\n        description=\"Strategy name in plain, everyday language — short and jargon-free so a non-expert grasps it at a glance and it fits the run visualizations. Aim for about 4-8 words (~40 characters).\"\n    )\n    objective: Annotated[str, LLMPrompt, LLMStructOut] = Field(\n        description=\"The novel contribution we're building toward\"\n    )\n    rationale: Annotated[str, LLMPrompt, LLMStructOut] = Field(\n        description=\"Why this strategy is promising\"\n    )\n    artifact_directions: Annotated[list[ArtifactDirection], LLMPrompt, LLMStructOut] = Field(\n        description=\"Artifacts to execute THIS iteration\"\n    )\n    expected_outcome: Annotated[str, LLMPrompt, LLMStructOut] = Field(\n        description=\"What we'll have after this iteration's artifacts complete\"\n    )\n    summary: Annotated[str, LLMPrompt, LLMStructOut] = Field(\n        default=\"\",\n        description=\"Brief summary of the strategy and its expected contribution\",\n    )\n\n\nclass Strategies(LLMPromptModel, LLMStructOutModel):\n    \"\"\"Top-level wrapper for LLM strategy generation output.\"\"\"\n\n    kind: Literal[\"strategies\"] = \"strategies\"\n    \"\"\"Wire-level discriminator for downstream consumers that narrow\n    polymorphic outputs by ``kind``. No LLMStructOut annotation:\n    invisible to the LLM's structured-output schema, populated via\n    the default at deserialize time.\"\"\"\n\n    strategies: Annotated[list[Strategy], LLMPrompt, LLMStructOut] = Field(\n        description=\"List of generated strategies\"\n    )\n\n\nclass GenStratOut(LLMPromptModel):\n    \"\"\"Aggregate output of gen_strat module with all strategies.\n\n    Every Strategy produced across the parallel tasks in one iteration.\n    Used as the typed payload for ``module_output(output=...)``; readers\n    walk ``module.output.strategies``.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    kind: Literal[\"gen_strat_out\"] = \"gen_strat_out\"\n    strategies: list[Strategy] = Field(default_factory=list)\n\n\n# =============================================================================\n# UTILITY: Assign artifact IDs\n# =============================================================================\n\n\ndef assign_artifact_direction_ids(\n    strategy: dict,\n    seen_ids: set[str],\n    iteration: int,\n) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Assign unique IDs to artifact directions.\n\n    The LLM does not generate IDs - we assign them sequentially based on type.\n    IDs are like 'experiment_iter1_dir1'.\n\n    IMPORTANT: Mutates seen_ids — new IDs are added so subsequent calls\n    (e.g., for other strategies in the same batch) won't generate duplicates.\n    Callers should pass a working copy if they need the original set unchanged.\n\n    Args:\n        strategy: Single strategy dict from LLM output\n        seen_ids: Mutable set of all known IDs (pool + previously assigned). Gets updated in place.\n        iteration: Current iteration number for ID generation\n\n    Returns:\n        Updated strategy with assigned IDs\n    \"\"\"\n    artifact_directions = strategy.get(\"artifact_directions\", [])\n\n    def _make_unique(base_id: str) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Make an ID unique by appending suffix if needed.\"\"\"\n        if base_id not in seen_ids:\n            seen_ids.add(base_id)\n            return base_id\n\n        # Find unique suffix\n        suffix_num = 1\n        while True:\n            new_id = f\"{base_id}_{suffix_num}\"\n            if new_id not in seen_ids:\n                seen_ids.add(new_id)\n                return new_id\n            suffix_num += 1\n\n    # Assign IDs\n    for i, artifact in enumerate(artifact_directions):\n        artifact_type = artifact.get(\"type\", \"unknown\")\n        # Create base ID: type_iter{N}_dir{M}\n        base_id = f\"{artifact_type}_iter{iteration}_dir{i + 1}\"\n        artifact[\"id\"] = _make_unique(base_id)\n\n    return strategy\n\n\n# =============================================================================\n# UTILITY: Validate artifact dependencies\n# =============================================================================\n\n# Dependency rules: artifact_type -> (required_dep_types, optional_dep_types)\n# required_dep_types: Must have at least one dependency of these types\n# optional_dep_types: Can have dependencies of these types (in addition to required)\nDEPENDENCY_RULES: dict[str, tuple[set[str], set[str]]] = {\n    \"experiment\": ({\"dataset\"}, {\"research\"}),  # REQUIRED: dataset, OPTIONAL: research\n    \"evaluation\": (\n        {\"experiment\"},\n        {\"dataset\"},\n    ),  # REQUIRED: experiment, OPTIONAL: dataset\n    \"dataset\": (set(), {\"research\"}),  # REQUIRED: none, OPTIONAL: research\n    \"research\": (set(), {\"research\"}),  # REQUIRED: none, OPTIONAL: research\n    \"proof\": (set(), {\"research\"}),  # REQUIRED: none, OPTIONAL: research\n}\n\n\ndef validate_artifact_dependencies(\n    strategy: dict,\n    artifact_pool_map: dict[str, str],\n) -> tuple[bool, list[str]]:\n    \"\"\"Validate that artifact dependencies are valid and follow the rules.\n\n    Checks:\n    1. All depends_on IDs exist in the artifact pool\n    2. Dependency types match the rules for each artifact type\n\n    Args:\n        strategy: Single strategy dict from LLM output\n        artifact_pool_map: Dict mapping artifact ID -> artifact type for all existing artifacts\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (is_valid, list of error messages)\n    \"\"\"\n    errors: list[str] = []\n    artifact_directions = strategy.get(\"artifact_directions\", [])\n\n    for artifact in artifact_directions:\n        artifact_id = artifact.get(\"id\", \"unknown\")\n        artifact_type = artifact.get(\"type\", \"unknown\")\n        depends_on = artifact.get(\"depends_on\", [])\n\n        # Get rules for this artifact type\n        required_types, optional_types = DEPENDENCY_RULES.get(artifact_type, (set(), set()))\n        allowed_types = required_types | optional_types\n\n        # Track which required types have been satisfied\n        found_required_types: set[str] = set()\n\n        for dep in depends_on:\n            dep_id = dep.get(\"id\", \"\") if isinstance(dep, dict) else getattr(dep, \"id\", \"\")\n            dep_label = dep.get(\"label\", \"\") if isinstance(dep, dict) else getattr(dep, \"label\", \"\")\n\n            # Check 0: Label must be a non-empty short string.\n            if not dep_label or not dep_label.strip():\n                errors.append(\n                    f\"Artifact '{artifact_id}': dependency '{dep_id}' has empty label — every dep needs a short type label\"\n                )\n\n            # Check 1: Does the dependency ID exist?\n            if dep_id not in artifact_pool_map:\n                errors.append(\n                    f\"Artifact '{artifact_id}': dependency '{dep_id}' does not exist in artifact pool\"\n                )\n                continue\n\n            # Check 2: Is the dependency type allowed?\n            dep_type = artifact_pool_map[dep_id]\n            if dep_type not in allowed_types:\n                errors.append(\n                    f\"Artifact '{artifact_id}' ({artifact_type}): dependency '{dep_id}' has type '{dep_type}' \"\n                    f\"which is not allowed (allowed: {allowed_types})\"\n                )\n            elif dep_type in required_types:\n                found_required_types.add(dep_type)\n\n        # Check 3: Are required dependencies satisfied?\n        # Skip if the pool has no artifacts of the required type (e.g., iteration 1: no datasets yet)\n        pool_has_required = any(atype in required_types for atype in artifact_pool_map.values())\n        if required_types and not found_required_types and pool_has_required:\n            errors.append(\n                f\"Artifact '{artifact_id}' ({artifact_type}): missing required dependency. \"\n                f\"Must have at least one dependency of type: {required_types}\"\n            )\n\n    is_valid = not errors\n    return is_valid, errors\n\n\n# =============================================================================\n# VERIFICATION: Verify strategies output\n# =============================================================================\n\n\ndef verify_strategies(\n    strategies: list[dict],\n    num_expected: int,\n    existing_artifact_ids: set[str],\n    artifact_pool_map: dict[str, str],\n    min_valid_artifacts: int = 1,\n    *,\n    allowed_artifacts: list[str] | None = None,\n    art_limit: int | None = None,\n) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Verify strategies against rules.\n\n    Returns dict with:\n    - valid: bool - True if all checks pass (including min_valid_artifacts)\n    - count_errors: list - Count mismatch errors\n    - id_errors: list - Duplicate ID errors\n    - dep_errors: list - Dependency errors\n    - type_errors: list - Invalid artifact type errors\n    - limit_errors: list - Artifact count exceeds art_limit\n    - strategies_received: int - Number of strategies received\n    - valid_artifact_count: int - Number of valid artifacts across all strategies\n    - total_artifact_count: int - Total artifacts across all strategies\n    - invalid_artifact_ids: set - IDs of invalid artifacts\n    \"\"\"\n    # Default allowed types if not specified\n    valid_types = (\n        set(allowed_artifacts)\n        if allowed_artifacts\n        else {\"research\", \"dataset\", \"proof\", \"experiment\", \"evaluation\"}\n    )\n\n    count_errors = []\n    id_errors = []\n    dep_errors = []\n    type_errors = []\n    limit_errors = []\n\n    # Track which artifact IDs are invalid (for counting valid artifacts)\n    invalid_artifact_ids: set[str] = set()\n    total_artifact_count = 0\n\n    # Check strategy count\n    if len(strategies) != num_expected:\n        count_errors.append(f\"Expected {num_expected} strategies, got {len(strategies)}\")\n\n    # Check each strategy\n    for s_idx, s_dict in enumerate(strategies):\n        artifact_directions = s_dict.get(\"artifact_directions\", [])\n\n        # Check artifact count limit per strategy\n        if art_limit is not None and len(artifact_directions) > art_limit:\n            limit_errors.append(\n                f\"Strategy {s_idx + 1} ('{s_dict.get('title', 'Untitled')}'): \"\n                f\"has {len(artifact_directions)} artifact directions but limit is {art_limit}\"\n            )\n\n        # First pass: collect ALL artifact IDs in this strategy\n        strategy_ids = set()\n        seen_ids = set()  # For duplicate detection within strategy\n        for a_dict in artifact_directions:\n            artifact_id = a_dict.get(\"id\", \"\")\n            artifact_type = a_dict.get(\"type\", \"\")\n            total_artifact_count += 1\n\n            # Check artifact type is allowed\n            if artifact_type not in valid_types:\n                type_errors.append(\n                    f\"Strategy {s_idx + 1}: artifact '{artifact_id}' has type '{artifact_type}' \"\n                    f\"which is not in allowed_artifacts: {sorted(valid_types)}\"\n                )\n                invalid_artifact_ids.add(artifact_id)\n\n            if artifact_id:\n                strategy_ids.add(artifact_id)\n\n                # Check for duplicate within same strategy\n                if artifact_id in seen_ids:\n                    id_errors.append(\n                        f\"Strategy {s_idx + 1}: duplicate artifact ID '{artifact_id}' within same strategy\"\n                    )\n                    invalid_artifact_ids.add(artifact_id)\n                seen_ids.add(artifact_id)\n\n                # Check for duplicate against existing pool\n                if artifact_id in existing_artifact_ids:\n                    id_errors.append(\n                        f\"Strategy {s_idx + 1}: artifact ID '{artifact_id}' already exists in artifact pool\"\n                    )\n                    invalid_artifact_ids.add(artifact_id)\n\n        # Second pass: check dependencies\n        for a_dict in artifact_directions:\n            artifact_id = a_dict.get(\"id\", \"\")\n            depends_on = a_dict.get(\"depends_on\", [])\n\n            for dep in depends_on:\n                dep_id = dep.get(\"id\", \"\") if isinstance(dep, dict) else getattr(dep, \"id\", \"\")\n                dep_label = (\n                    dep.get(\"label\", \"\") if isinstance(dep, dict) else getattr(dep, \"label\", \"\")\n                )\n\n                # Every dep must have a non-empty short label.\n                if not dep_label or not dep_label.strip():\n                    dep_errors.append(\n                        f\"Strategy {s_idx + 1}: artifact '{artifact_id}' has dependency on '{dep_id}' with empty label \"\n                        f\"— every dep needs a short type label (a word or two)\"\n                    )\n                    invalid_artifact_ids.add(artifact_id)\n\n                # Check if depends_on references another artifact in SAME strategy (invalid - they run in parallel)\n                if dep_id in strategy_ids:\n                    dep_errors.append(\n                        f\"Strategy {s_idx + 1}: artifact '{artifact_id}' depends on '{dep_id}' \"\n                        f\"which is in the SAME strategy (artifacts run in parallel, can't depend on each other)\"\n                    )\n                    invalid_artifact_ids.add(artifact_id)\n                # Check if depends_on references an ID that doesn't exist anywhere\n                elif dep_id not in existing_artifact_ids:\n                    dep_errors.append(\n                        f\"Strategy {s_idx + 1}: artifact '{artifact_id}' depends on '{dep_id}' \"\n                        f\"which does not exist in artifact pool\"\n                    )\n                    invalid_artifact_ids.add(artifact_id)\n\n        # Validate dependency TYPES against artifact pool rules (e.g., experiments require datasets)\n        is_valid, errors = validate_artifact_dependencies(s_dict, artifact_pool_map)\n        if not is_valid:\n            for err in errors:\n                dep_errors.append(f\"Strategy {s_idx + 1}: {err}\")\n                # Mark artifact as invalid based on error message (extract artifact ID if possible)\n                # The error format is: \"Artifact 'artifact_id': ...\"\n                if \"Artifact '\" in err:\n                    try:\n                        art_id = err.split(\"Artifact '\")[1].split(\"'\")[0]\n                        invalid_artifact_ids.add(art_id)\n                    except (IndexError, ValueError):\n                        # Message didn't match the quoted-id shape — the error is\n                        # already in dep_errors; only per-artifact marking is skipped.\n                        pass\n\n    # Count valid artifacts — use a counter, not set length, because duplicate IDs\n    # map to multiple artifacts but only one set entry\n    invalid_count = sum(\n        1\n        for s_dict in strategies\n        for a_dict in s_dict.get(\"artifact_directions\", [])\n        if a_dict.get(\"id\", \"\") in invalid_artifact_ids\n    )\n    valid_artifact_count = total_artifact_count - invalid_count\n\n    # Check if we have enough valid artifacts\n    has_enough_valid = valid_artifact_count >= min_valid_artifacts\n\n    # Overall valid: no errors AND enough valid artifacts\n    valid = (\n        not count_errors\n        and not id_errors\n        and not dep_errors\n        and not type_errors\n        and not limit_errors\n        and has_enough_valid\n    )\n\n    return {\n        \"valid\": valid,\n        \"count_errors\": count_errors,\n        \"id_errors\": id_errors,\n        \"dep_errors\": dep_errors,\n        \"type_errors\": type_errors,\n        \"limit_errors\": limit_errors,\n        \"strategies_received\": len(strategies),\n        \"valid_artifact_count\": valid_artifact_count,\n        \"total_artifact_count\": total_artifact_count,\n        \"invalid_artifact_ids\": invalid_artifact_ids,\n    }\n", "numLines": 1027, "startLine": 1, "totalLines": 1027}}
